2026-10-22T02:28:39.9322804Z   UV_CACHE_DIR: /home/runner/work/_temp/setup-uv-cache
2026-10-22T02:28:39.9370984Z   QWEN_COMMIT_MESSAGE: [require-build] [cluster:aspire2ap] [build-only] Build the image with an opt-in small-M SM90 host geometry for Qwen Decode FP8 projections (DG_SM90_1D2D_SMALL_M)

Why:
At 128 verification rows the real DeepGEMM host selects 64x144 QKV and 64x64 O layouts, each with 128 useful tiles on the 132-SM H200. At 129 through 144 rows the third M tile changes QKV to a B-multicast 64x112 layout with 249 logical tiles and two waves, and O to 64x96 with nonuniform weight scales. A single M tile with narrower N tiles can test whether weight reuse and stable single-wave scheduling remove this overhead. The original BLOCK_M128 occupancy hypothesis was contradicted by the native heuristic reproduction and serving kernel names.

What:
Add the independent host patch sm90-1d2d-small-m.patch after the existing two host patches in the parent image-build series. Only when DG_SM90_1D2D_SMALL_M is nonzero, select BLOCK_M256/BLOCK_K128, BLOCK_N80 for N9216/K4096 or BLOCK_N32 for N4096/K8192. Restrict selection to normal 1d2d GEMMs with 65 through 144 rows, one group, 132 SMs, K-major E4M3 A and B, BF16 output and no accumulation. All other shapes and the unset/zero switch retain the stock search. Prefill projection choices remain unchanged. No header overlay or MegaMoE header change is required.

The existing pipeline calculation gives QKV four stages and 217600 bytes dynamic shared memory, O five stages and 206336 bytes, 128 TMA threads and 256 math threads, and no multicast. There are 116 QKV and 128 O logical tiles, one wave, at M128 through M144. BN80 uses the stock per128 weight-scale boundary handling; BN32 uniformly indexes the original scale group. All K blocks and requested output elements are computed with unchanged FP8 operands, original FP32 scales and FP32 accumulation, followed by BF16 conversion. Extra padded arithmetic is retained.

Checks:
The source is pristine sgl-project DeepGEMM v0.1.5 commit 1c8138a59b3df5dac50b76b730834ed57982fdfb. Both existing host patches and this independent patch apply in sequence. The full csrc/tvm_ffi_api.cpp passes g++ C++17 syntax-only compilation using local torch, tvm-ffi and CUDA13 headers, with no errors and only preexisting torch C++20 bitfield warnings. A native CPU heuristic executable reproduces the recorded baseline and verifies candidate and unaffected M boundaries including Prefill4096.

On the Firmus login node, CPU nvcc13.0.88 from image39693217189 compiled four recorded Decode controls, the two candidates and unchanged Prefill using the installed image header, SM90a and register-usage-level10. Both candidates use168 registers with zero stack and zero spill loads/stores. The controls use248 registers without spills. The unchanged Prefill template has168 registers,40-byte stack and36-byte spill loads/stores. The installed 1d2d header SHA256 is8667552aeb0f56be360bf71bcaeacd1d252cfc0cd12a7c555ae747e68bb64e8d, identical to upstream. Candidate cubin SHA256 values: QKV522e53c3a1236fd15daff4758b680467eb62387419569434f7a9c96d6471c3ef; O9d47a12cdf57613caf5419d66601defbbf19addc82aa1af74a3e8e895ca268b1.

Expected:
This contributes one host patch to the combined image build without enabling it. A later kernel benchmark explicitly measures stock and candidate switches on that same rebuilt image, then serving can enable it only on Decode after validation. Public v0.1.5 APIs cannot directly force the candidate layout on the old image. No extra build variation, branch or scheduler action was used during preparation.

Risks:
CPU syntax/instantiation checks do not link/load the rebuilt extension or establish numeric correctness or speed. The native cost model predicts30403 QKV and50964 O cycles for the padded candidate versus18671 and22669 at M128, and padding may lose. The rough unmeasured target is13 to18 microseconds QKV and11 to16 O at M128/M132/M144; keep it disabled until actual GPU screening and controlled timings justify a serving experiment. Official accuracy, result parsing and goodput remain unverified. No GPU jobs, commits, pushes or branch operations were performed. The named Firmus scratch directory was made writable and removed after collecting compiler artifacts.
